/system script add dont-require-permissions=no name=sgp-aviso owner=sgp policy=ftp,reboot,read,write,policy,test,password,sniff,sensitive source=":log info \"sgp aviso\";\r\
    \n/file remove [find where name=sgp_aviso.rsc]\r\
    \n/tool fetch url=\"http://170.80.59.103:8000/ws/mikrotik/aviso/pendencia/\\\?token=366ac1a7-5226-4689-9108-92f530d1487e&app=mikrotik\" dst-path=sgp_aviso.rsc;\r\
    \n:delay 30s\r\
    \nimport file-name=sgp_aviso.rsc;\r\
    \n:delay 10s;\r\
    \n/ip firewall address-list set timeout=00:15:00 [/ip firewall address-list find list=BLOQUEIO-AVISAR]"
/system script add dont-require-permissions=no name=sgp_login_local owner=sgp policy=ftp,reboot,read,write,policy,test,password,sniff,sensitive,romon source=":global filename \"sgp_login_local.txt\";\r\
    \n/file remove [/file find name=\$filename]\r\
    \n/tool fetch url=\"http://170.80.59.103:8000/ws/mikrotik/login/local/\\\?token=366ac1a7-5226-4689-9108-92f530d1487e&app=mikrotik&disabled=1\" duration=30 dst-path=\$filename;\r\
    \n:delay 32s;\r\
    \n/import file-name=\$filename;"
/tool netwatch add comment="ATIVAR O SECRETS CASO O RADIUS PARE" disabled=no down-script="/ppp secret ; :foreach i in [ find comment~\"SGP:\" ] do={ enable \$i }; /ppp active; :foreach p in [find \\ radius=no comment~\"SGP:\"] do={ remove \$p; :delay 1};" host=170.79.93.122 interval=3m timeout=10s type=simple up-script="/ppp secret ; :foreach i in [ find comment~\"SGP:\" ] do={ disable \$i }; /ppp active; :foreach p in [find \\ radius=no comment~\"SGP:\"] do={ remove \$p; :delay 1};"
/tool netwatch add comment="SW-PM05-MAJOR PORTO" disabled=no down-script=":put [/interface vlan enable [find where vlan-id=127]]\r\
    \n:put [/interface vlan enable [find where vlan-id=129]]\r\
    \n:put [/interface vlan enable [find where vlan-id=173]]\r\
    \n\r\
    \n" host=10.43.43.1 http-codes="" test-script="" type=simple up-script=":put [/interface vlan disable [find where vlan-id=127]]\r\
    \n:put [/interface vlan disable [find where vlan-id=129]]\r\
    \n:put [/interface vlan disable [find where vlan-id=173]]"

    \n:put [/interface vlan enable [find where vlan-id=129]]\r\
